### [95] [On Waring--Goldbach problem with mixed powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12397)
*Huimin Wang,Shuangrui Tian*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了每个充分大的奇数n都可以表示为7个素数幂的和：p₁²+p₂²+p₃³+p₄⁴+p₅⁵+p₆⁶+p₇⁸，改进了Cai和Mu在2015年的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在改进Cai和Mu（2015年）的已有成果，进一步放宽或优化素数幂混合Waring-Goldbach问题中的条件，证明更一般形式的下述方程对所有充分大的奇数可解。

Method: 本文采用解析数论中的圆法（Hardy-Littlewood方法），结合素数幂分布的指数和估计技术，对所涉及的算术级数上的素数幂求和进行精确估计，从而建立该方程的可解性。

Result: 证明了对于所有充分大的奇数n，方程n=p₁²+p₂²+p₃³+p₄⁴+p₅⁵+p₆⁶+p₇⁸存在素数解(p₁,...,p₇)，这是对Cai和Mu（2015年）相应结果的改进。

Conclusion: 该结果扩展了素数幂Waring-Goldbach问题中混合幂次的适用范围，表明在较弱的幂次组合下方程仍可解，为进一步研究更一般的混合幂次素数方程提供了理论基础。

Abstract: It is proved that for every sufficiently large odd integer $n$, the equation $$ n=p_1^2+p_2^2+p_3^3+p_4^4+p_5^5+p_6^6+p_7^8 $$ is solvable in primes. This refines the work of Cai and Mu in 2015.

</details>


### [96] [Generalized Addition Theorems for the Jacobi Elliptic Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12399)
*Efe Gürel*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广了Jacobi椭圆函数的经典加法定理，给出了大量隐式和显式公式，包括行列式恒等式、单函数显式加法公式，以及新颖的4、5、6、7项加法公式和一般的n项加法公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jacobi椭圆函数的加法定理是经典数学中的重要工具，此前已有一些经典结果，但缺乏更一般化的推广。本文旨在系统地推广这些加法定理，涵盖更多项和更一般的形式。

Method: 通过构造涉及多个Jacobi椭圆函数的行列式恒等式，在此基础上推导显式加法公式，并进一步推广到4项、5项、6项、7项乃至一般的n项加法公式。

Result: 得到了涉及多个Jacobi函数的行列式恒等式，证明了针对单个函数的显式加法公式，并给出了新颖的4-、5-、6-和7-项加法公式，以及一般的n项加法公式。

Conclusion: 本文系统地推广了Jacobi椭圆函数的加法定理，为任意项数的加法公式提供了统一的框架，丰富了该领域的理论工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we generalize the classical addition theorems for the Jacobi elliptic functions, giving numerous implicit and explicit formulas. We first present determinant identities involving several of the Jacobi functions. Then, we prove explicit addition formulas for a single function. Novel 4-,5-,6- and 7-term addition formulas are also given alongside a general $n$-term addition formula.

</details>


### [97] [A conditional arithmetic obstruction to the prime numbers as a spectrum of a probability measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12400)
*Zhi-Yi Wu,Qian Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：如果每个足够大的正偶数都能表示为两个素数之差（即波利尼亚克猜想的一种弱化形式），则素数集合P不能作为任何R上Borel概率测度μ的L²(μ)空间的指数正交基（即谱）。因此，在波利尼亚克猜想成立的前提下，素数集不可能构成任何概率测度的谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨素数集合是否可以作为某种概率测度的Fourier谱（即指数正交基），这一问题将数论（素数差分布）与调和分析（谱测度理论）联系起来。作者试图建立素数差表示偶数的性质与谱存在性之间的否定性联系。

Method: 通过假设每个足够大的正偶数均可表示为两个素数的差，利用这一数论条件推导出矛盾，从而否定素数集合作为L²(μ)中指数正交基的可能性。证明采用反证法，将谱的正交完备性与素数之差的性质（波利尼亚克猜想）相结合。

Result: 证明了在给定的数论假设（足够大正偶数均可表示为两素数之差）下，素数集合P不能成为任何R上Borel概率测度的L²空间的指数正交基。特别地，若波利尼亚克猜想成立，素数集P不可能是任何概率测度的谱。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了波利尼亚克猜想（一个著名的数论猜想）与调和分析中谱测度问题之间的深刻联系，表明若素数的差集覆盖所有足够大的偶数，则素数集不具备作为任何测度谱的资格。这一发现为理解谱集的结构提供了新的数论视角。

Abstract: Let $\Pp=\{2,3,5,7,\ldots\}$ denote the set of prime numbers. We prove that if every sufficiently large positive even integer can be represented as a difference of two primes, then there is no Borel probability measure $μ$ on $\R$ for which \(\left\{e^{2πi p x}:p\in\Pp\right\}\) is an orthonormal basis of $L^2(μ)$. In particular, under the Polignac conjecture, the prime numbers $\Pp$ cannot be a spectrum (i.e., the set of frequencies of an exponential orthonormal basis) of any probability measure on $\R$.

</details>


### [98] [Finding all cospectral mates over a number field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12410)
*Alexander Van Werde*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了由代数数域参数化的整数矩阵的共谱性概念，给出了谱确定性的充分条件，并利用判别式和Krylov子空间的性质来约束正交矩阵，同时开发了在给定小域上寻找所有共谱伙伴的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数矩阵的特殊完全图形理论中，研究矩阵的谱确定性问题是一个经典课题。作者将这一概念推广到代数数域参数化的框架下，探索何时可以对整数矩阵进行域限定下的正交共轭从而产生新的整数矩阵，这为图的谱确定性问题提供了新的视角和工具。

Method: 本文利用特征多项式的判别式与Krylov子空间的性质来推导谱确定性的充分条件；当这些条件不适用时，推导了正交矩阵所受的约束；并在此基础上设计了在给定小域上枚举所有共谱伙伴的算法，该算法有公开实现。

Result: 给出了关于域参数化共谱性下谱确定性的充分条件，以及在条件不满足时对正交矩阵的约束；提出了一个有效算法用于在小域上寻找所有共谱伙伴，并提供了实现。

Conclusion: 本文将整数矩阵的共谱性概念推广到代数数域参数的框架，通过判别式和Krylov子空间建立了充分条件与约束，并提供了实用算法，为谱确定性问题提供了新的理论工具和计算方法。

Abstract: We investigate a notion of cospectrality for integer matrices that is parameterized by algebraic number fields. Given a number field and a symmetric integer matrix, we wonder when conjugating the integer matrix by an orthogonal matrix with entries in the given field can produce new integer matrices.
  Our results concern sufficient conditions for the associated notion of spectral determination, and we give constraints on the orthogonal matrices when the conditions are not applicable. The results use the discriminant of the characteristic polynomial and properties of Krylov subspaces. We leverage the theory to develop an algorithm to find all cospectral mates over a given (small) field. An implementation of the algorithm is made available.

</details>


### [99] [Special Values of Shifted Dirichlet Series from an Adjoint Map on Almost Holomorphic Modular Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12542)
*Raveena Ganash*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文找到了几乎全纯模形式空间上某个映射的伴随算子，该映射与拟模形式空间上的广义Ramanujan-Serre导数类似。作为应用，作者显式给出了与拟模形式相关的某些移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值，并证明了该移位Dirichlet级数的有理性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模形式理论中，Ramanujan-Serre导数在拟模形式空间上的作用已有深入研究，但其伴随算子的构造以及对相关Dirichlet级数的特殊值研究尚待完善。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立几乎全纯模形式空间上的伴随理论，并将其应用于移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值与有理性研究。

Method: 作者在几乎全纯模形式空间上构造了与拟模形式空间上广义Ramanujan-Serre导数相伴随的映射，利用伴随算子的性质来研究移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值，并通过解析和代数方法证明其有理性。

Result: 文中显式求出了与拟模形式相关的某些移位Dirichlet级数在特殊点处的值，并证明了这些移位Dirichlet级数的有理性结论。

Conclusion: 本文建立了几乎全纯模形式与拟模形式之间的伴随理论联系，并由此得到了移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值公式和有理性结果，丰富了模形式与Dirichlet级数之间的联系。

Abstract: We find the adjoint of a map on the space of almost holomorphic modular forms, analogous to the generalized Ramanujan-Serre derivative on the space of quasi-modular forms. As applications, we explicitly find special values of certain shifted Dirichlet series associated to quasi-modular forms and prove a rationality result for the shifted Dirichlet series.

</details>


### [100] [Density bounds for permutations avoiding monotone arithmetic progressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12604)
*Jesse Geneson*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了整数集和自然数集上允许ω-置换且不含单调ℓ项算术级数的子集的上、下密度上确界。证明了α_N(3)≥2/3和α_Z(3)≥2/3，并构造了四可置换的整数子集使其下对称密度趋近于1，从而证明β_Z(4)=1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在改进现有的关于不含单调三项算术级数的ω-置换子集的上密度下界，并探索四可置换的整数子集的下密度极限，以填补该领域已有的上下界之间的空白。

Method: 通过具体的构造性证明方法：对自然数和整数子集，构造特定的子集结构以证明其上密度至少达到2/3；对于β_Z(4)=1，通过构造四可置换的整数子集，使其下对称密度趋近于1。

Result: 成功证明了α_N(3)≥2/3和α_Z(3)≥2/3，改进了文献中已有的下界；同时通过构造证明了β_Z(4)=1。

Conclusion: 该研究显著加强了关于不含单调三项算术级数的ω-置换子集的密度下界，并在四可置换情形下给出了完全刻画，推进了该领域对密度参数的认识。

Abstract: For $X\in\{\mathbb{N},\mathbb{Z}\}$, let $α_X(\ell)$ and $β_X(\ell)$ denote the supremal upper and lower densities of subsets of $X$ admitting $ω$-permutations without monotone $\ell$-term arithmetic progressions. We strengthen the published lower bounds for the three-term upper-density parameters by proving \[
  α_{\mathbb{N}}(3)\geq\frac23,\qquad
  α_{\mathbb{Z}}(3)\geq\frac23. \] We also prove $β_{\mathbb{Z}}(4)=1$ by constructing four-permutable subsets of the integers whose lower symmetric densities approach one.

</details>


### [101] [Escalations and criteria over real quadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12648)
*Jakub Krásenský,Giuliano Romeo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究完全实数域上普适二次形式的判别准则集。通过发展数域上的"升级法"（escalation），理论证明了判别准则的有限性，并在Q(√2)和Q(√3)上进行了显式计算，获得普适性准则的猜想；同时针对对角二次形式发展了一套不同的升级理论，得到Q(√5)的对角准则集以及对Q(√2)和Q(√3)的猜想结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有理数域上，15-定理和290-定理完全刻画了普适二次形式，类似的定理虽存在于每个完全实数数域上，但除Q(√5)外判别准则集均未知，因此需要发展计算方法来求得更一般数域上的普适性判别准则。

Method: 将经典的"升级法"推广到数域上，提供一个普适性准则有限性的简洁证明，并将其作为计算准则集的实用工具；此外为对角二次形式发展一套独立的升级理论。

Result: 在Q(√2)和Q(√3)上进行了显式计算，得到相应的普适性准则猜想；证明了许多升级格的普适性；对Q(√5)得到了对角准则集，对Q(√2)和Q(√3)得到对角准则集的猜想。

Conclusion: 数域上的升级法兼具理论价值（证明准则有限性）与实用价值（计算准则集），为完全实数数域上普适二次形式的刻画提供了新工具，并在多个具体数域上产生了可验证的猜想结果。

Abstract: The famous 15-Theorem and 290-Theorem fully characterise universal quadratic forms over $\mathbb{Q}$. Similar theorems exist for every totally real number field, but only over $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt5)$ the criterion set is explicitly known. We study criterion sets both theoretically and computationally: We develop the method of escalation over number fields, thus providing a simple proof of finiteness of the criteria and, more importantly, a practical tool for computing them. We illustrate this by explicit computations for $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt2)$ and $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt3)$, obtaining a conjecture about the corresponding universality criteria; we also prove universality of many escalator lattices. Moreover, we develop the somewhat different theory of escalations for diagonal quadratic forms, obtaining the diagonal criterion set for $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt5)$ and conjecturally for $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt2)$ and $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt3)$.

</details>


### [102] [Algebraic constructions of point sequences with quasi-uniform two-dimensional projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12690)
*Takashi Goda*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文基于计算机实验中的序贯空间填充设计背景，研究d维单位立方体中可扩展点集的代数构造，使所有二维坐标投影均为准均匀分布。通过三次数域构造显式Kronecker序列、通过实二次域构造嵌套rank-1格设计，并利用代数范数估计与对偶丢番图逼近的转置原理，证明了分离半径下界和覆盖半径上界均为点数的最优阶，且对全域坐标对一致成立。同时引入射影线P¹(Q)上有限构型的minimax问题以优化网格比率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序贯空间填充设计需要可逐步扩展的点集，且要求低维投影保持空间填充性能。现有构造难以同时满足所有二维坐标投影的准均匀性，需要新的代数方法系统构造此类点集并给出最优阶的定量保证。

Method: 提出两种共享同一参数化（P¹(Q)上有理方向有限构型）的构造：(1) 利用三次数域构造显式Kronecker序列，使得所有二维坐标投影的网格比率在每段初始长度N≥2上一致有界；(2) 利用实二次域、分裂素数及相容p-adic嵌入构造嵌套rank-1格设计，在每一嵌套层级保持均匀投影性质。证明综合代数范数估计与同时/对偶丢番图逼近之间的转置原理，分别得到分离半径和覆盖半径的最优阶下界与上界。

Result: 两种构造均保证了所有二维坐标投影的分离半径与覆盖半径达到点数的最优阶，且网格比率一致有界。此外将有理射影系数的选择归结为P¹(Q)上有限构型的minimax问题，刻画如何最小化所有二维投影中最大网格比率，最终提供具有统一控制双变量几何性质的扩展点集。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于数域与射影几何的扩展点集系统构造框架，统一了Kronecker序列与嵌套格设计两类方法，为序贯空间填充设计提供了具有最优阶且全二维投影均匀的理论保障，并开辟了关于射影有理构型的极小极大优化问题这一新研究方向。

Abstract: Motivated by sequential space-filling designs for computer experiments, we study algebraic constructions of extensible point sets in the $d$-dimensional unit cube whose two-dimensional coordinate projections are all quasi-uniform. Our two constructions share a common parametrization in terms of finite configurations of distinct rational directions on the projective line $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{Q})$. First, using a cubic number field, we construct explicit Kronecker sequences for which the mesh ratios of all two-dimensional coordinate projections remain uniformly bounded over every initial segment of length $N\ge 2$. Second, using a real quadratic field, a split prime, and a compatible $p$-adic embedding, we construct nested rank-1 lattice designs with the same uniform projection property at every nesting level. The proofs combine algebraic norm estimates with transference principles between simultaneous and dual Diophantine approximation, yielding lower bounds for the separation radii and upper bounds for the covering radii, both of optimal order in the number of points, uniformly over all coordinate pairs. We also investigate how the choice of rational projective coefficients affects the resulting mesh ratios. This leads to a minimax problem for finite configurations on $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{Q})$, in which one seeks to minimize the maximum mesh ratio over all two-dimensional coordinate projections. These constructions provide extensible point sets with uniformly controlled bivariate geometry.

</details>


### [103] [Uniform bounds on prime levels of abelian division fields of elliptic curves over number fields without rationally defined CM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12709)
*Hansol Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文去除了广义黎曼猜想（GRH）假设，证明了艾伦和赫纳奥先前在GRH下得到的关于椭圆曲线n-分圆域为阿贝尔域的素数层结果，实际上等价于椭圆曲线不存在有理数定义的复乘法（CM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: González-Jiménez和Lozano-Robledo证明了定义在Q上的椭圆曲线其n-分圆域为阿贝尔域的层数n存在一致上界。Allen和Genao在广义黎曼猜想（GRH）假设下将该结果部分推广到素数层且允许任意数域（无有理数定义CM）。本文的目标是去掉GRH假设，并在无条件的设定下建立该性质与无CM之间的等价性。

Method: 本文通过移除GRH假设，采用无条件证明方法，建立了一个更强的等价性刻画：椭圆曲线的素数阶分圆域为阿贝尔域这一性质，当且仅当椭圆曲线没有有理数定义的复乘法。这需要绕开依赖GRH的工具（如有效楚米-切博塔列夫界或相关解析数论结果），改用无条件算术几何方法进行分析。

Result: 成功去除了GRH假设，证明Allen和Genao所建立的性质与不存在有理数定义的CM是等价的，即该性质是椭圆曲线无CM（在此特定意义下）的充要条件。

Conclusion: 该结果在不依赖任何未证假设的情况下给出了椭圆曲线分圆域阿贝尔性与复乘法存在性之间的精确等价关系，完善并强化了此前在GRH下获得的部分结果。

Abstract: Enrique Gonz{á}lez-Jim{é}nez and {Á}lvaro Lozano-Robledo proved that there exists a uniform bound on the levels $n$ for which the $n$-division field $\mathbb{Q}\left(E\left[n\right]\right) / \mathbb{Q}$ of an elliptic curve $E/\mathbb{Q}$ defined over $\mathbb{Q}$ is abelian. Assuming GRH (Generalized Riemann Hypothesis), Allen and Genao partially generalized this result by restricting to prime levels while allowing arbitrary number fields without rationally defined CM. In this paper, we remove the GRH assumption and prove that the property established by Allen and Genao is in fact equivalent to the absence of rationally defined CM.

</details>


### [104] [Finite-Sum Realization of Archimedean Asai and Exterior-Square $L$-Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12722)
*Yeongseong Jo,Akash Yadav*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了阿基米德Asai L-因子可表示为Flicker局部zeta积分的有限和，同时证明GL_m(F)的外平方L-因子可表示为Jacquet-Shalika局部zeta积分的有限和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将L-因子与局部zeta积分联系起来是朗兰兹纲领中的核心问题。作者希望证明在阿基米德情形下，Asai L-因子和外平方L-因子都能通过具体的局部zeta积分来实现，从而深化对这些L-因子的解析理解。

Method: 作者针对阿基米德局部域F上的不可约generic表示进行研究：（1）对GL_n(C)的generic表示，证明其Asai L-因子可分解为Flicker局部zeta积分的有限和；（2）对GL_m(F)的generic表示，证明其外平方L-因子可分解为Jacquet-Shalika局部zeta积分的有限和。所采用的方法涉及阿基米德表示的解析性质和局部zeta积分的理论。

Result: 成功建立了Asai L-因子与Flicker局部zeta积分之间的联系，以及外平方L-因子与Jacquet-Shalika局部zeta积分之间的联系，均为有限和的表达式。

Conclusion: 本文为阿基米德情形的Asai L-因子和外平方L-因子提供了具体的积分表达式，这些结果对于理解相关L-因子的解析延续、函数方程及特殊值等性质具有重要意义。

Abstract: We prove that the archimedean Asai $L$-factor attached to an irreducible generic representation of $\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ can be expressed as a finite sum of Flicker local zeta integrals. For an archimedean local field $F$, we also prove that the exterior-square $L$-factor attached to an irreducible generic representation of $\operatorname{GL}_m(F)$ can be expressed as a finite sum of Jacquet--Shalika local zeta integrals.

</details>


### [105] [On the Lorenz-Fibonacci sequences and substitutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12739)
*Bernardo San Martín,Víctor Sirvent*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了两族整数序列——第一类和第二类(k,r)-Lorenz-Fibonacci序列，其特征多项式为p_{k,r}(x)=x^k-x^{k-1}-...-x^r+x^{r-1}+...+x+1（其中k≥2r+1且k≥3）。当r=0时，这些序列退化为著名的k-bonacci序列。这些序列源于Lorenz吸引子动力学研究。文章引入两族与上述整数序列相关联的字母表置换(substitutions)，两者共享同一特征多项式，并研究了这些置换的主要组合性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这些整数序列在Lorenz吸引子的动力学研究中自然涌现，理解其对应的组合结构（置换）有助于揭示动力系统背后的符号动力学特性，同时推广了经典的k-bonacci序列及相关置换理论。

Method: 定义第一类和第二类(k,r)-Lorenz-Fibonacci整数序列，基于其特征多项式建立递推关系；构造两族在k符号字母表上的置换(substitutions)，使其分别与两类整数序列相关联，并通过分析置换的迭代结构来研究其组合性质。

Result: 成功引入两族与(k,r)-Lorenz-Fibonacci序列对应的置换，验证了置换与整数序列共享同一特征多项式，并给出了这些置换的主要组合性质（如长度增长、结构特征等）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了(k,r)-Lorenz-Fibonacci整数序列与置换之间的对应关系，将Lorenz吸引子动力系统中的数值序列推广到符号动力学层面，为后续研究这类置换的谱性质、子位移性质等提供了基础。

Abstract: In the present article, we consider two families of integer sequences, the $(k,r)$-Lorenz-Fibonacci sequences of the first and second kind, whose characteristic polynomial is $$p_{k,r}(x)=x^k-x^{k-1}-\cdots- x^r+x^{r-1}+\cdots +x+1,$$ where $k\geq 2r+1$, and $k\geq 3$. These families include the well-known $k$-bonacci sequence, when $r=0$. These sequences arise naturally in the study of the dynamics of the Lorenz attractor. We introduce two families of substitutions (in an alphabet of $k$ symbols) so that they are associated with each of the families of integer sequences and share the same polynomial. We study the main combinatorial properties of these substitutions.

</details>


### [106] [Cyclic permutations of large subsets with polynomial values in multiplicative subgroups of finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12758)
*Hai-Liang Wu,He-Xia Ni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上多项式值的k次幂剩余构成哈密顿圈的阈值问题。对于非零判别式的整系数多项式f(t)和整数k≥2，当素数p≡1(mod k)足够大时，建立了阈值c(p,k,f)：任何满足#A≥c(p,k,f)的集合A⊆F_p都存在一个排列，使得相邻两项之和f(a_i+a_{i+1})均为F_p^*中的k次幂。同时给出了该最小可能阈值的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于有限域上的组合数论与图论的交叉，研究在有限域中寻找具有特定幂剩余性质的全循环排列（哈密顿圈）。此类问题与加性组合学、指数和估计以及谱图理论密切相关，具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 论文综合运用了有限域上的混合指数和（mixed exponential sums）、离散傅里叶分析以及谱图理论三种工具。首先利用混合指数和来估计相关图结构的边分布，再通过离散傅里叶分析控制偏差，最后借助谱图理论论证图中哈密顿圈的存在性。

Result: 对于充分大的素数p≡1(mod k)，建立了使集合A中所有元素可排列成满足f(a_i+a_{i+1})为k次非零幂剩余的首尾相接排列的阈值c(p,k,f)，并给出了该最小可能阈值c(p,k,f)的下界与上界估计。

Conclusion: 论文在一般多项式框架下推广了有限域上k次幂剩余哈密顿圈的存在性问题，提供了显式的阈值界，揭示了几何对象（广义Paley图类）中哈密顿圈存在的密度条件。

Abstract: Let $f(t)\in\mathbb{Z}[t]$ be a nonconstant polynomial with nonzero discriminant and let $k\ge2$ be an integer. For every
  sufficiently large prime $p\equiv1\pmod{k}$, by applying mixed exponential sums over finite fields, discrete Fourier analysis and the spectral graph theory, we establish a threshold $c(p,k,f)$ such that any subset $A\subseteq\mathbb F_p$ with $\#A\ge c(p,k,f)$ admits a permutation $a_1, a_2,\cdots, a_{\#A}$ of $A$ satisfying $f(a_i+a_{i+1})\in\{x^k: x\in\mathbb{F}_p^*\}$ for any $1\le i \le \#A$, where $a_{\#A+1}=a_1$. Also, we give lower and upper bounds for the least possible threshold.

</details>


### [107] [A study of degenerate Bernoulli and Euler numbers via operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12927)
*Taekyun Kim,Dae san Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入新的算子类别，并将其应用于特殊数的研究，推导出退化欧拉数和第二类退化欧拉数的显式表达式，同时建立了退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过引入新的算子来系统地研究退化特殊数（如退化欧拉数、退化伯努利数），为这些数的显式表达式和相互关系的推导提供统一的数学工具。

Method: 提出新颖的算子类别，利用这些算子推导退化欧拉数和第二类退化欧拉数的显式表达式，并建立退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的关联。

Result: 成功获得了退化欧拉数和第二类退化欧拉数的显式表达式，并确立了退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的数学关系。这些算子是在研究上述表达式和关系时自然涌现的。

Conclusion: 新引入的算子为退化特殊数的研究提供了有力工具，不仅简化了显式表达式的推导，还揭示了退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的内在联系。

Abstract: The paper introduces novel classes of operators and investigates their applications to the study of special numbers. We utilize these operators to derive explicit expressions for degenerate Euler numbers and type 2 degenerate Euler numbers. Furthermore, we establish a relationship between degenerate Bernoulli numbers and degenerate Euler numbers. We note here that these operators emerge naturally when we study such explicit expressions and such a relationship

</details>


### [108] [Analytic rank one propagation in Hida families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13145)
*Stefano Vigni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：在特定算术几何猜想（p进Abel-Jacobi映射的单射性、Gillet-Soulé高度配对的正定性）成立的前提下，对非CM新形式f（权重k≥4，平凡Nebentypus），几乎所有使f为ordinary的奇素数p，若f的解析秩为1，则Hida族中几乎所有偶权重平凡Nebentypus特化也具有解析秩1。这为Greenberg的"极小性猜想"提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Greenberg的"极小性猜想"预言模形式族中解析秩的分布应取极小值。此前该猜想缺乏系统证据，尤其需要将单一点f的解析秩信息推广到整个Hida族的所有特化。作者希望建立f的解析秩与其p进Hida族各特化解析秩之间的传递关系。

Method: 假设两个算术代数几何中的一般猜想：(1) p进Abel-Jacobi映射的单射性；(2) archimedean高度配对的Gillet-Soulé正定性。在此前提下，利用p进Hida族理论，将f的解析秩为1的信息沿Hida族传播到几乎所有偶权重平凡Nebentypus特化上，证明这些特化的解析秩也为1。

Result: 对所有但有限多个满足条件的素数p，若f的解析秩为1，则Hida族中几乎所有偶权重平凡Nebentypus特化（除有限多个例外）的解析秩也为1。

Conclusion: 该结果从解析侧支持了Greenberg关于模形式族中解析秩分布的极小性猜想，表明在Hida族中解析秩1的"极小性"在特化间具有广泛的传递性。

Abstract: Let $f$ be a non-CM newform of weight $k\geq4$, level $N$ and trivial Nebentypus. Let $p\nmid N$ be an odd prime number that is ordinary for $f$ and denote by $\boldsymbol f^{(p)}$ the $p$-adic Hida family passing through $f$. Assuming very specific instances of two general conjectures in arithmetic algebraic geometry (injectivity of $p$-adic Abel-Jacobi maps, positive definiteness of archimedean height pairings à la Gillet-Soulé), we prove that, for all but finitely many $p$ as above, if the analytic rank of $f$ is $1$, then all but finitely many specializations of $\boldsymbol f^{(p)}$ of even weight and trivial Nebentypus have analytic rank $1$. This result provides evidence for Greenberg's "minimality conjecture" on analytic ranks in families of modular forms.

</details>


### [109] [On $\mathcal{B}^4$-almost periodicity for a class of arithmetical functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13172)
*Kritika Aggarwal,Debika Banerjee,Shubham Gupta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对Chandrasekharan-Narasimhan引入的一类广泛算术函数的正规化误差项，建立了Besicovitch意义下的B^4-殆周期性，并由此推导出该误差项具有极限概率分布及显式的四次均值矩公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往文献中仅研究了相关误差项的B^2-殆周期性，本文旨在将其显著加强为B^4-殆周期性，从而获得更精细的分布性质和矩信息。

Method: 通过推导截断的Voronoï型公式，证明正规化误差项属于Besicovitch空间B^4。

Result: 证明了正规化误差项的B^4-殆周期性；作为推论，得到了误差项存在极限概率分布，并建立了其四次均值矩的显式公式。

Conclusion: 本文的结果将此前仅有的B^2-殆周期性推广到B^4-殆周期性，为理解此类算术函数误差项的分布规律提供了更深入的刻画。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the $\mathcal{B}^4$-almost periodicity in the sense of Besicovitch for a suitably normalized error term associated with a broad class of arithmetical functions introduced by Chandrasekharan and Narasimhan. This result significantly strengthens $\mathcal{B}^2$-almost periodicity previously investigated in the literature for related error terms. By deriving truncated Voronoï-type formulas, we demonstrate that the normalized error term lies in the Besicovitch space $B^4$. As a consequence, we deduce that the error term admits a limit probability distribution and establish an explicit formula for its mean fourth power moment.

</details>


### [110] [Generalizations of the Christoffel-Darboux formula and congruences involving Apéry-like numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13192)
*Zhi-Hong Sun*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文首先将正交多项式中的Christoffel-Darboux公式推广到一般的三项递推序列，然后研究了由两个递推关系定义的多项式$g_n(x)$和$v_n(x)$的恒等式与同余性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Christoffel-Darboux公式是正交多项式理论中的核心恒等式，将其推广到更一般的三项递推序列可以扩展其应用范围，并有助于分析特定多项式序列（如$g_n(x)$和$v_n(x)$）的恒等与同余性质。

Method: 首先将Christoffel-Darboux公式推广到一般三项递推序列，然后利用该推广结果对由给定递推关系定义的多项式$g_n(x)$（系数含$(n+1)^2$）和$v_n(x)$（系数含$(n+1)^3$）进行恒等式推导与同余性质研究。

Result: 获得了推广的Christoffel-Darboux公式，并由此推导出$g_n(x)$和$v_n(x)$相关的恒等式及同余结果。

Conclusion: 推广的Christoffel-Darboux公式为研究一般三项递推序列派生的多项式恒等式与同余性质提供了有效工具，并为$g_n(x)$和$v_n(x)$两类特殊多项式序列建立了新的代数性质。

Abstract: In this paper, we first extend the Christoffel-Darboux formula for orthogonal polynomials to general three-term recurrence sequences, and then investigate the identities and congruences for $g_n(x)$ and $v_n(x)$ given by \begin{align*} &g_0(x)=1,\ g_1(x)=\frac{x+1}2,\ (n+1)^2g_{n+1}(x)=\Big(2n(n+1)+\frac{x+1}2\Big)g_n(x)-n^2g_{n-1}(x)\ (n\ge 1), \\&v_0(x)=1,\ v_1(x)=x,\ (n+1)^3v_{n+1}(x)=(2n+1)(n(n+1)+x)v_n(x)-n^3v_{n-1}(x)\ (n\ge 1).\end{align*}

</details>


### [111] [Infinitely many elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ of exact ranks 4 and 6 with $j$-invariant 1728](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13202)
*Ben Savoie*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为每个$r\in\{4,6\}$在$\mathbb{Q}(i)$上构造了显式的单参数椭圆曲线族，其$j$-不变量为1728且秩恰好为$r$，包含无穷多彼此不同构的曲线。通过构造$\mathbb{Q}(i)$-有理点给出秩下界，利用Kai关于数域上线性模式素数值的定理配合$[1+i]$-下降得到匹配的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在椭圆曲线秩理论中，构造具有精确给定秩且$j$-不变量固定的曲线族是一个经典难题。作者此前完成了秩2的构造，本文旨在将策略推广到更高的秩4和6，探究固定$j$-不变量的椭圆曲线秩的可能范围，并揭示秩与纠错码理论（如Reed-Muller码）之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者将此前秩2论文中对称高斯素数构型替换为满足若干互补平方恒等式的二元线性形式系统。通过显式构造$\mathbb{Q}(i)$-有理点获得秩的下界；利用Kai关于数域上线性模式素数值的定理实现具有受控局部行为的特化，从而通过$[1+i]$-下降证明秩的上界。在秩6情形中，三个构造点与$(0,0)$的支撑向量张成自对偶Reed-Muller码$\mathrm{RM}(1,3)$，该码同时也是控制Selmer群的二次剩余Laplacian的核。

Result: 成功为$r=4$和$r=6$构造了显式的单参数椭圆曲线族，每条曲线都定义在$\mathbb{Q}(i)$上，$j$-不变量为1728，秩精确等于$r$，且族中包含无穷多对彼此不同构的曲线。秩6的构造还揭示了椭圆曲线Selmer群与Reed-Muller码$\mathrm{RM}(1,3)$之间的密切联系。

Conclusion: 本文成功将作者此前秩2的构造策略推广到秩4和6，在$\mathbb{Q}(i)$上建立了$j$-不变量固定为1728的高秩椭圆曲线族的显式构造方法。秩6情形中Selmer群与Reed-Muller码的对应关系为椭圆曲线算术与编码理论之间架起了桥梁，为进一步研究更高秩的构造提供了新思路。

Abstract: For each $r\in\{4,6\}$, we construct an explicit one-parameter family of elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ containing infinitely many pairwise nonisomorphic curves genuinely defined over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ with $j$-invariant $1728$ and rank exactly $r$. We construct explicit $\mathbb{Q}(i)$-rational points to bound the ranks from below. Kai's theorem on prime values of linear patterns over number fields provides specializations with controlled local behavior, allowing us to obtain matching upper bounds via $[1+i]$-descent. The construction extends the strategy of the author's earlier rank-$2$ paper by replacing a symmetric Gaussian-prime configuration with systems of binary linear forms satisfying several complementary square identities. In the rank-$6$ case, the support vectors attached to the three constructed points and $(0,0)$ span the self-dual Reed-Muller code $\mathrm{RM}(1,3)$, which also occurs as the kernel of the quadratic-residue Laplacian governing the Selmer group.

</details>


### [112] [Asymptotic Analysis and Phase Transition of the Bessel-Kuznetsov Transform with an Oscillatory Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13232)
*Yuhang Shi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对GL(2)上的Bessel-Kuznetsov变换在高度振荡线性相位e(αx)下的半经典极限t→∞进行了严格的渐近分析，通过将WKB近似应用于虚阶Bessel核，揭示了依赖于扭转参数α的尖锐相变：当α≤1/2π时变换快速衰减，而当α>1/2π时产生O(t^{-1})量级的局部主项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典界仅能保证光滑非振荡测试函数下Bessel-Kuznetsov变换的快速衰减，但现代解析数论应用（特别是涉及扭曲移位卷积和的问题）中经常遇到具有高度振荡线性相位e(αx)的测试函数，需要对这种振荡条件下的变换行为提供严格的渐近分析。

Method: 将WKB近似方法应用于GL(2) Kuznetsov迹公式中出现的虚阶Bessel核，在t→∞的半经典极限下对Bessel-Kuznetsov积分变换进行显式渐近展开，并通过分析几何振荡与谱核之间的共振关系来刻画相变行为。

Result: 识别出以α=1/2π为临界点的尖锐相变：在亚临界区域（α≤1/2π），变换快速衰减；在超临界区域（α>1/2π），几何振荡与谱核产生共振，得到具有简化算术相位的O(t^{-1})量级局部主项。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了振荡测试函数下Bessel-Kuznetsov变换渐近行为的理论空白，为解析数论中扭曲移位卷积和等应用提供了严格的工具支撑。

Abstract: The spectral side of the Kuznetsov trace formula for $GL(2)$ is governed by the Bessel-Kuznetsov integral transform $\checkφ(t)$. While classical bounds guarantee rapid decay of this transform for smooth, non-oscillatory test functions, modern applications in analytic number theory---particularly those involving twisted shifted convolution sums---frequently encounter test functions exhibiting a highly oscillatory linear phase $e(αx)$. In this paper, we provide a rigorous and explicit asymptotic analysis of $\checkφ(t)$ in the semiclassical limit $t \to \infty$ under such oscillatory conditions. By applying the WKB approximation to the imaginary-order Bessel kernel, we identify a sharp phase transition dependent on the twist parameter $α$. We prove that in the sub-critical regime ($α\le 1/2π$), the transform decays rapidly. Conversely, in the super-critical regime ($α> 1/2π$), the geometric oscillations resonate with the spectral kernel, yielding a localized main term of order $O(t^{-1})$ with a remarkably simplified arithmetic phase.

</details>


### [113] [On a conjecture of Corradi and Katai](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13266)
*Krishnarjun Krishnamoorthy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了与Liouville函数相关的Goldbach型求和，证明了其中存在充分的抵消性，并将其应用于Liouville函数的符号模式研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldbach猜想是数论中的经典问题，而Liouville函数作为重要算术函数，其相关的Goldbach型求和及其抵消性质的研究有助于深入理解素数与Liouville函数的分布规律。同时，Liouville函数的符号模式也是解析数论中的活跃研究课题。

Method: 通过对Liouville函数相关的Goldbach型求和进行分析，证明其中存在足够的抵消（cancellations），并将这些结果应用于研究Liouville函数的符号模式（sign patterns）。

Result: 证明了Liouville函数对应的Goldbach型求和存在充分的抵消性，并由此得到了关于Liouville函数符号模式的应用结果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Goldbach型求和中Liouville函数抵消性的新结果，并成功将其与Liouville函数的符号模式问题联系起来。

Abstract: We consider Goldbach type sums corresponding to the Liouville function and prove the existence of sufficient cancellations. We also consider applications to sign patterns in the Liouville function.

</details>


### [114] [Convolution-type Bombieri-Vinogradov theorem with well-factorable Weights, and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13299)
*Zhiyuan Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用Pascadi的三重良因子卷积估计和不完全Kloosterman和估计，将Bombieri-Friedlander-Iwaniec的素数算术级数等分布结果推广到卷积形式，并以此改进了与最大素因子相关的两个计数问题的下界和上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Bombieri-Friedlander-Iwaniec结果（1986）在模数至多x^{4/7-ε}的算术级数中得到素数等分布，作者希望将其推广到卷积形式，以获得更强的解析数论工具，并应用于涉及最大素因子P^+(n)的两个计数问题的估计。

Method: 应用Pascadi提出的三重良因子（triply-well-factorable）卷积估计以及不完全Kloosterman和的估计，将BFI结果推广为卷积形式；再利用这一卷积形式的等分布结果分别处理关于P^+(n)和P^+(p-1)的渐近密度与极限上界问题。

Result: 证明了#{n≤x:P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)}>0.296x（改进了作者此前0.280的结果），并证明了#{p≤x:P^+(p-1)≥p^c}的极限上界不超过分段函数S(c)，其中S(c)<(7/2)log(1/c)，改进了Ding和Wang（2025）的结果。

Conclusion: 通过将BFI型等分布推广到卷积形式，结合Pascadi的估计技术，在两类与最大素因子相关的计数问题上取得了优于以往工作的定量结果。

Abstract: In 1986, Bombieri, Friedlander and Iwaniec famously obtained that primes are equidistributed in arithmetic progressions to moduli up to $x^{4/7-\varepsilon}$, using well-factorable weights. In this paper, we apply Pascadi's triply-well-factorable convolution estimate and his estimation of incomplete Kloosterman sums to generalize this result to a convolutionform, which improves Wang's result under certain conditions. As for application, we consider the asymptotic density of $\#\{n\leq x:P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)\}$ and $\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}$, where $P^+(n)$ denote the largest prime factor of $n$. We show that for $x\rightarrow\infty$, one has\begin{align*}
  \#\{n\leq x:P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)\}>0.296x
  \end{align*}
  and
  \begin{align*}
  &\mathop{\lim\sup}_{x\rightarrow\infty} \frac{1}{π(x)}\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}\\&\leq S(c)=\left\{ \begin{aligned}
  & \int_0^{1-c}\frac{2}{(5/8-189u/200)(1-u)}\mathrm{d}u, \quad&& \frac{184}{189}\leq c<1,\\& S\left(\frac{184}{189}\right)+\frac{10}{3}\log\frac{184}{189c},\quad&& 0.7404<c\leq \frac{184}{189},
  \end{aligned}\right.
  \end{align*}
  where the function $S(c)$ satisfties $S(c)<\frac{7}{2}\log\frac{1}{c} $ for $0.7404<c<1$. The first result improves a previous result $0.280$ by the author (2026). The second result constitutes an improvement upon that of Ding and Wang (2025), who obatined $\mathop{\lim\sup}_{x\rightarrow\infty} \frac{1}{π(x)}\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}\leq \frac{7}{2}\log\frac{1}{c}$.

</details>


### [115] [Local Minkowski units in non-abelian extensions with cyclic Sylow $p$-subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13312)
*Wan Lee,Donghyeok Lim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个判定准则，用于确定在伽罗瓦扩张（其伽罗瓦群为非同p-群与循环p-群的直积）中局部Minkowski单位在p处存在的充要条件，并将其应用于构造非阿贝尔扩张及分析数域的Zp-扩张中单位的Iwasawa模结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究局部Minkowski单位的存在性是一个重要的数论问题，此前的结果可能仅适用于特殊类型的伽罗瓦群。本文旨在推广到更一般的伽罗瓦群结构（非同p-群与循环p-群的直积），并探索其在Iwasawa理论和分歧条件下的应用。

Method: 建立关于局部Minkowski单位存在性的判据，适用于伽罗瓦群为非同p-群与循环p-群直积的所有伽罗瓦扩张；利用该判据在多种分歧条件下构造具有局部Minkowski单位的非阿贝尔扩张；进一步分析数域的Zp-扩张中单位群的Iwasawa模结构，并将研究扩展到p次单位根群非平凡的情形。

Result: 给出了局部Minkowski单位存在性的完整判据；构造了多种分歧条件下具有局部Minkowski单位的非阿贝尔扩张；刻画了Zp-扩张中单位的Iwasawa模结构；并将结果推广到p次单位根群非平凡的情形。

Conclusion: 本文系统地推广了局部Minkowski单位存在性的判定理论，覆盖了更广泛的伽罗瓦群类型，并在Iwasawa理论中建立了新的应用，同时处理了p次单位根群非平凡的推广情形。

Abstract: We establish a criterion for the existence of a local Minkowski unit at $p$ that applies to all Galois extensions with Galois group isomorphic to the direct product of a non-$p$-group and a cyclic $p$-group. As applications, we construct non-abelian extensions admitting a local Minkowski unit at $p$ under various ramification conditions and analyze the Iwasawa module structure of units in $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extensions of number fields. We also extend our study to the case where the group of $p$-power roots of unity is nontrivial.

</details>


### [116] [Some convolution identities for mock modular forms arising from the theory of holomorphic projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13324)
*Jonathan G. Bradley-Thrush,Frank Garvan,Jayashree Kalita,Larry Rolen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文发展了全纯投影的新结果，推广了Imamoğlu-Raum-Richter关于调和Maass形式与全纯模形式乘积的全纯投影理论，将适用范围从广义Pell方程D为平方数的有限情形推广到一般D（含无穷多解的情形），并作为应用解决了第二作者的一个猜想，得到了18个mock theta函数卷积恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积恒等式和递推公式在全纯模形式理论及其组合学应用中长期扮演重要角色。近年来，非全纯模形式（如调和Maass形式）产生的此类公式引起新的兴趣，包括Hurwitz-Kronecker类数关系及Imamoğlu-Raum-Richter（IRR）的开创性工作。IRR的理论仅在广义Pell方程m²-Dn²=N中D为平方数时适用，此时对给定N仅有有限多个解。本文旨在发展更便捷的框架，覆盖一般D的情形（尤其Pell方程有无穷多解的情况）。

Method: 本文推广了Imamoğlu-Raum-Richter的全纯投影理论，发展出适用于一般D（而非仅限于D为平方数）的新结果。该新框架更便于处理许多自然情形。作为应用，通过该全纯投影方法，作者推导出18个mock theta函数的卷积恒等式，并将其化简为5个可直接证明其余恒等式的核心恒等式。附录中还用更直接的q级数方法展示了这些恒等式的证明。

Result: 本文的主要结果覆盖了一般D的情形，特别是Pell方程有无穷多解的情形。作为应用，解决了第二作者近期提出的一个猜想。此外，通过新方法获得了18个mock theta函数卷积恒等式，其中5个为基本恒等式，可用于推导其余13个。附录提供了这些恒等式的直接q级数证明。

Conclusion: 全纯投影公式的核心价值在于提供了一种自动发现和验证此类卷积恒等式的工具，而不仅仅限于特定情形的证明。本文的工作将IRR理论从有限解的平方数情形扩展到一般D，拓展了这一工具的应用范围。

Abstract: Convolution identities and recursive formulas have long played a role in the theory of holomorphic modular forms and their applications. These have served both as striking formulas and as fundamentally useful tools for applications to combinatorics. Recently, there has been renewed interest in examples arising from non-holomorphic modular forms. These include the famous Hurwitz-Kronecker class number relations dating to 1885, and groundbreaking work of Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter from 2014. Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter developed a theory of holomorphic projection for products of (vector-valued) harmonic Maass forms and holomorphic modular forms to produce many such formulas. This was related shortly thereafter by Duncan, Griffin, and Ono to replicable-type functions in the sense of Conway and Norton, and such recursions played a key role in their proof of the Umbral Moonshine Conjecture.
  Here, we develop new results on holomorphic projections of such functions which is more convenient for many natural cases. Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter's choice corresponds to the case in which the generalized Pell equation $m^2 - Dn^2 = N$ has a square value for $D$, which has only finitely many solutions for a given value of $N$. Our main results cover the cases of general $D$, in particular those for which the Pell equation has infinitely many solutions.
  As an application, we resolve a recent conjecture of the second author. More generally, our approach yields 18 convolution identities for mock theta functions, which we boil down to 5 identities that can directly be used to prove the others. In the appendices, we also show how these identities can be proven by more direct $q$-series methods; however, the key utility of the holomorphic projection formulas is that they give a tool to automatically discover and verify such formulas.

</details>


### [117] [Gross vectors modulo 2 and elliptic curves of prime conductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13371)
*Matija Kazalicki,Siniša Slijepčević*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明超奇异椭圆曲线上的Gross向量（模2约化）张成整个F_2向量空间，并由此推导出Kazalicki-Kohen猜想和Watkins猜想对特定椭圆曲线族成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是理解超奇异椭圆曲线在特征p中的奇偶结构，并建立Brandt特征向量系数奇偶性与椭圆曲线秩（rank 0 vs 正秩）之间的代数联系，从而为秩零提供一个纯代数的"证书"（certificate）。

Method: 关键步骤是将Gross三元格表示数的奇偶性约化到Frobenius垂直的二秩子格上的表示，利用Ibukiyama显式极大序将所得的原始二元型与Xiao-Zhou-Deng-Qu参数化中的形式对应起来，再结合类域论与Chebotarev定理逐一隔离超奇异坐标的奇偶性。

Result: 证明了(m_i(D) mod 2)跨越F_2^{S_p}；由此推出正Mordell-Weil秩的椭圆曲线（素数导子）其Brandt特征向量所有系数均为偶数，证实Kazalicki-Kohen猜想；结合Mestre和Gross-Kudla公式，证明根数与+1的正秩素数导子曲线的模度被4整除，进而对这类秩2曲线证明Watkins猜想。

Conclusion: 奇奇偶系数成为秩0的代数证书；奇偶性定理与模度可除性之间的桥梁为秩与算术不变量的关系提供了新的技术手段。

Abstract: Let p > 3 be a prime, and let S_p denote the geometric isomorphism classes of supersingular elliptic curves in characteristic p whose j-invariants lie in F_p. For each negative fundamental discriminant -D for which p is inert in Q(sqrt(-D)), let m_i(D), i in S_p, be the integral coefficients of the corresponding Gross vector. We prove that the vectors (m_i(D) mod 2)_{i in S_p} span F_2^{S_p}. The key step reduces the parity of the representation numbers of Gross's ternary lattices to representation by rank-two sublattices perpendicular to Frobenius. Using Ibukiyama's explicit maximal orders, the resulting primitive binary forms are identified with those occurring in the Xiao--Zhou--Deng--Qu parametrization of supersingular elliptic curves over F_p. Class field theory and Chebotarev's theorem then allow the individual supersingular coordinates to be isolated.
  As a consequence, if E/Q has prime conductor p and positive Mordell--Weil rank, then every coefficient of its Brandt eigenvector indexed by S_p is even, proving a conjecture of Kazalicki and Kohen. Thus an odd coefficient at a rational supersingular class is an algebraic certificate of rank 0. Combining this parity theorem with formulas of Mestre and Gross--Kudla, we also prove that the modular degree of every positive-rank elliptic curve of prime conductor and root number +1 is divisible by 4. Consequently, Watkins' conjecture holds for all such curves of rank 2.

</details>


### [118] [Intersective Polynomials and Universal Separation of Divosor Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13388)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对互素多项式对的通用除数谱分离进行了分类，确定了任意次数互素多项式对以及所有次数不超过2的多项式对的分离准则。关键在于"交截性"（intersective）条件：互素的F,G之间通用无界分离当且仅当其中之一是交截的。对于含公共不可约因子的对，通用分离迫使UV在几乎所有素数模下有根，并完全解决了次数≤2情形下的有限p-adic边界问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究除数函数d_A(F(n))与d_A(G(n))在不同多项式F,G下的分离行为，是数论中关于因子分布与多项式取值互动的核心问题。此前对任意次数的互素多项式对的完整分类尚属空白，对有公共因子的多项式对的p-adic边界条件也未被完全刻画，本文旨在填补充这些理论空白。

Method: 将多项式按照互素与含公共不可约因子两类情形分别处理。对互素情形，证明通用无界分离等价于其中一个多项式为交截的（即有模任何正整数的根），并通过引入多个多项式对手的"一步式优势"推广到更一般情形。对有公共因子的情形，以U,V表示仅发生在两侧的因子乘积，证明分离迫使UV在几乎所有素数下模有根（等价于其Galois作用无极位置换），再用均匀几乎素数取值于根等差级数的方法结合自适应的局部路由论证，解决次数≤2情形的有限p-adic边界。

Result: 完整分类了任意次数互素多项式对的通用除数谱分离问题；证明了互素对通用无界分离当且仅当其中之一为交截多项式；对有公共因子的对，给出了UV的Galois无置换条件，并完全解决了次数≤2的所有情形：分离恰好当UV和F,G中至少一个为交截时成立，且该准则不受内容或因子重数影响。该准则对任意次数的三个线性支撑因子（任意正整数重数）同样成立。

Conclusion: 通过统一几乎素数取值技术与自适应局部路由方法，完整刻画了通用除数谱分离的准则。核心结论是：分离的判定可归结为交截性的组合条件，且这一准则在次数≤2情形下是充要的，体现了多项式整除结构与局部可解性之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We classify universal divisor-profile separation for coprime polynomial pairs of arbitrary degree and for all pairs of degree at most two. For \(A\subset\N\) and \(m\in\Z\), let \(d_A(m)\) count the members of \(A\) dividing \(m\). For coprime nonzero \(F,G\in\Z[x]\), universally unbounded separation between \(d_A(F(n))\) and \(d_A(G(n))\) occurs if and only if one of \(F,G\) is intersective, that is, has a root modulo every positive integer. More generally, an intersective factor separated from finitely many polynomial opponents yields simultaneous one-sided dominance against all of them.
  For pairs with common irreducible factors, let \(U,V\) be the products of the factors occurring only on the two respective sides. Universal separation forces \(UV\) to have a root modulo almost every prime; equivalently, its Galois action has no derangement. We resolve the remaining finite \(p\)-adic boundary for all pairs of degree at most two: separation holds exactly when \(UV\) and at least one of \(F,G\) are intersective, and the criterion is unchanged by contents or factor multiplicities. The same criterion holds, in arbitrary degree, for three linear support factors with arbitrary positive multiplicities. The proofs combine uniform almost-prime values on root progressions with an adaptive local-routing argument.

</details>


### [119] [The distribution of $k$-free ideals in ray class groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13509)
*Adrian Barquero-Sanchez,Jack Heimrath,Bernd Sing,Nicolás Sirolli,Caylee Spivey,Michael Wijaya*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用射线类群的语言，将算术级数中k-free整数分布这一经典问题推广到任意数域的设定下，建立了k-free理想在有界范数且属于给定射线类时的渐近公式及误差项，证明了k-free理想在射线类间均匀分布，并通过Landau的计数渐近改进了理想类群和窄类群情形下的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典问题中，k-free整数在算术级数中的分布已被广泛研究（Gegenbauer、Cohen-Robinson等），但此前的工作（如Benkowski、Nymann、Sittinger）仅推广到数域的某些特定类群情境，未在更一般的射线类群框架下系统地研究k-free理想的渐近分布。本文的动机在于填补这一空白，将结果推广到任意数域及射线类群的完整设置，并获得更精确的误差估计。

Method: 本文采用解析数论与代数数论相结合的方法。首先引入射线类群的语言来描述k-free理想所属的算术等价类；然后利用Dedekind zeta函数的解析性质及Landau的理想的渐近计数结果，通过围道积分/Tauber型技巧推导渐近公式及其误差项。在理想类群和窄类群情形下，使用更精细的Landau理想计数渐近以获得改进的误差估计。最后通过显式计算实例验证渐近公式和均匀分布性质。

Result: 建立了任意数域中k-free理想在给定射线类内的渐近公式（含误差项）；证明了k-free理想在射线类间均匀分布；在理想类群和窄类群情形下利用Landau的计数渐近获得了改进的误差估计；恢复了Q上Gegenbauer和Cohen-Robinson的经典公式，并将其推广到射线类群设置，扩展了既有工作。

Conclusion: 本文在射线类群的统一框架下完整解决了k-free理想在任意数域算术类中的分布问题，不仅推广了经典和现有结果，还获得了更优的误差项，并提供了计算实例佐证理论结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend the classical problem of studying the distribution of $k$-free integers in arithmetic progressions to the setting of arbitrary number fields. Using the language of ray class groups, we establish asymptotic formulas, together with error terms, for the number of $k$-free ideals of bounded norm lying in a given ray class. In particular, our results show that $k$-free ideals are equidistributed among ray classes. We also obtain improved error estimates in the cases of ideal class groups and narrow class groups by using sharper ideal counting asymptotics due to Landau. Our results recover the classical formulas of Gegenbauer and Cohen--Robinson over $\mathbb{Q}$ and extend previous work of Benkowski, Nymann, and Sittinger to the setting of ray class groups. We also present explicit computational examples that illustrate the asymptotic formulas and the equidistribution of $k$-free ideals among ray classes.

</details>

### [120] [Magnus mountains on spinning neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12378)
*Yashaswi Gangwar,David Ian Jones*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了中子星弹性壳层中钉扎的超流涡旋施加的非轴对称Magnus力如何形成"Magnus山"（恒星形变），这种形变可产生连续引力波。通过考虑可压缩双组分恒星模型，在无限长圆柱近似下自洽求解流体与弹性组分的运动方程，发现电流四极矩为零，而无量纲质量四极矩可达~10^-5的量级，其大小受涡旋钉扎强度和壳层破裂应变的限制。如此大的形变对当前及未来探测器的连续引力波探测极具前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星上由钉扎超流涡旋产生的非轴对称Magnus力可能导致恒星形变，这种形变恒星可作为连续引力波源。研究这一机制的形变幅度对评估连续引力波的可探测性具有重要意义。

Method: 采用可压缩双组分恒星模型，考虑流体区、薄海洋和壳层，在无限长圆柱近似下自洽求解流体与弹性组分的耦合运动方程，从而确定恒星形变和质量四极矩。分别考察电流（磁流体）四极矩和质量四极矩。

Result: 电流四极矩为零；Magnus力产生的无量纲质量四极矩可达~10^-5量级，其上限受限于涡旋钉扎的有限强度和弹性壳层的破裂应变。

Conclusion: Magnus力可产生显著的中子星形变（质量四极矩~10^-5），对当前及未来引力波探测器而言是颇具前景的连续引力波源，值得在更真实的恒星模型上开展进一步研究。

Abstract: We investigate the formation of a "Magnus mountain'' on a neutron star, arising from the non-axisymmetric Magnus force acting on the elastic crust by pinned superfluid vortices. Such a deformed star would act as a source of continuous gravitational waves. We consider a compressible two-component stellar model and solve the coupled equations of motion for the fluid and elastic components self-consistently, allowing the stellar deformation and the resulting mass quadrupole to be determined. For simplicity, we model the star as an infinitely long cylinder, consisting of a fluid region with a thin ocean and crust. We find that the current quadrupole is zero, while the Magnus forces are strong enough to produce (dimensionless) mass quadrupoles as large as $\sim 10^{-5}$, and so would be limited only by the finite strength of the vortex pinning and the breaking strain of the star's elastic crust. Such large deformations are promising from the point of view of the detection of continuous gravitational waves by current and future detectors, and motivate further work on more realistic stellar models.

</details>


### [121] [Synchrotron and Inverse-Compton Signatures of Black to White Hole Explosions: Weibel-Mediated External Shocks in the Interstellar Medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12380)
*Hoang Van Quyet*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞-白洞转变抛射的相对论性近各向同性电jet与星际介质相互作用产生的非热电磁对应体（余辉），基于Weibel不稳定性介导的碰撞无碰撞前向激波，计算了其同步辐射与同步自康普顿辐射。结果表明该余辉持续时间仅十分之几秒到几十秒，比传统长暴余辉快数个数量级，且具有快速、微弱、近似各向同性、无消色差喷流拐折等特征，可能区别于短暴、磁星巨耀发及其他快速暂现源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞-白洞转变是一种量子引力预言的瞬态现象，其相对论性抛射与星际介质相互作用会产生电磁对应体。理解这类快速暂现源的非热余辉特征，有助于将之与短伽马暴、磁星巨耀发等已知天体物理事件区分开来，并为量子引力模型提供观测检验途径。

Method: 作者在先前的光球层模型基础上，将可用的动能参数化为$\epsilon_{\rm iso}=R_k E_{\gamma,\rm prompt}$。采用Weibel介导的激波场模型，在一区Thomson regime近似下计算冲击加速电子的同步辐射和SSC辐射，同时纳入逆康普顿冷却、Klein-Nishina抑制、自吸收和γ-γ不透明度等效应，精度为量级或一阶水平。文中还给出1 keV光子计数可探测性估计。

Result: 对于示例参数（$E_{\gamma,\rm prompt}\sim10^{44}$ erg，$R_k=0.1$，$\Gamma_0=100$），电子处于慢冷却状态；对$p=2.5<3$，同步辐射谱在冷却频率$\nu_c$处达到峰值，落在MeV到GeV波段并随时间软化为更低的频率。余辉持续时间仅约0.1至几十秒，比同能量、密度和洛伦兹因子的长暴余辉快数个量级。余辉的持续时间、峰值能量和通量对$R_k$、$n_{\rm ISM}$及微物理参数$\epsilon_e,\epsilon_B,\xi_e$高度敏感。文章识别出某些参数区域中，低于本征峰值的1 keV光子计数超过标称探测阈值。

Conclusion: 这一场景的观测特征——硬谱瞬发闪光之后的快速、微弱、近似各向同性暂现，且无消色差喷流拐折——可将其与短GRB、磁星巨耀发和其他快速暂现源区分开来。但作者同时指出前向激波仅为动力学、一区Thomson regime辐射近似以及示例性可探测性判据等假设限制了该模型的定量精度。

Abstract: We study the non-thermal electromagnetic counterpart expected when the relativistic, near-isotropic ejecta of a black-to-white hole transition sweep up the interstellar medium (ISM). Building on a photospheric model for the prompt $γ$-ray emission~\cite{Villani2026}, we parametrize the kinetic energy available for an external shock, $\eiso=R_k\egprompt$, and model the resulting collisionless forward shock with a Weibel-mediated field~\cite{Weibel1959,MedvedevLoeb1999,SironiSpitkovsky2011}. We estimate the synchrotron and synchrotron self-Compton (SSC) emission from shock-accelerated electrons in a one-zone, Thomson-regime approximation, including inverse-Compton cooling, Klein-Nishina suppression, self-absorption, and $γ$-$γ$ opacity, all at order-of-magnitude or first-order accuracy. For illustrative parameters ($\egprompt\sim10^{44}$ erg, $R_k=0.1$, $Γ_0=100$), the electrons are slow-cooling, so for $p=2.5<3$ the synchrotron spectrum peaks at the cooling frequency $ν_c$, in the MeV-to-GeV range and softening with time; the afterglow evolves in a fraction of a second to tens of seconds -- orders of magnitude faster than a classical long-duration GRB afterglow at comparable energy, density, and Lorentz factor -- with duration, peak energy, and flux depending sensitively on $R_k$, $\nism$, and $\epse,\epsB,\xie$. We identify parameter regions where a 1 keV photon-count estimate, below the intrinsic peak, exceeds a nominal detection threshold, and discuss features -- a fast, faint, roughly isotropic transient after a hard-spectrum prompt flash, without an achromatic jet break -- that may distinguish this scenario from short GRBs, magnetar giant flares, and other fast transients, while flagging the assumptions (forward-shock-only dynamics, one-zone Thomson-regime radiation, an illustrative detectability criterion) limiting its quantitative reach.

</details>


### [122] [Microscopic Interaction versus Purely Gravitational Coupling in Strange Quark Stars Admixed with Dark Matter: A One-Fluid and Two-Fluid Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12383)
*J. Sedaghat,G. H. Bordbar,M. Haghighat,S. M. Zebarjad*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了与标量暗物质混合的奇异夸克星，重点比较微观相互作用与纯引力耦合的作用。结果表明，相互作用的单流体模型能产生满足当前脉冲星质量和GW170817潮汐形变约束的可行构型（M_TOV > 2M_sun），而非相互作用的双流体模型则能更好地满足现有GW170817限制及下一代引力波探测器预期的更严格Lambda约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索奇异夸克星与标量暗物质混合的天体物理模型，理解暗物质微观相互作用与引力耦合对恒星可观测量（质量、半径、潮汐形变率）的影响，为下一代引力波探测器提供理论预测。

Method: 对比两种模型：（1）相互作用的单流体模型（暗物质与夸克物质存在微观相互作用）；（2）非相互作用的双流体模型（仅纯引力耦合）。通过计算不同类型奇异夸克星的质量-半径关系、最大质量（M_TOV）及潮汐形变率（Lambda），并与脉冲星M-R观测数据和GW170817引力波事件的Lambda约束进行比较。

Result: 相互作用的单流体模型可产生M_TOV > 2M_sun的可行构型，满足当前脉冲星M-R测量及GW170817的Lambda约束；而非相互作用的双流体模型在满足现有GW170817限制的同时，还能满足下一代引力波探测器预期的更严格Lambda上限。

Conclusion: 暗物质与奇异夸克物质的耦合方式显著影响星体的可观测性质。虽然两种模型均能产生与当前观测一致的天体，但非相互作用的双流体模型对潮汐形变率的预言更具前瞻性，能够满足下一代引力波探测器更严格的约束，这可能为区分暗物质与奇异夸克物质的相互作用机制提供观测途径。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate strange quark stars (SQSs) admixed with scalar dark matter (DM), focusing on the role of microscopic interactions versus purely gravitational coupling. Our results demonstrate that while the interacting one-fluid model yields viable configurations with M_TOV > 2M_sun that satisfy current pulsar M-R measurements alongside the tidal deformability (Lambda) constraints from GW170817, the noninteracting two-fluid model more robustly meets both existing GW170817 limits and the tighter Lambda bounds anticipated from next-generation gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [123] [Interacting Supernovae: a Radio and X-ray Strategy to Constrain the Structure of the Circumstellar Medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12464)

TL;DR: 本文研究了超新星抛射物与非球形星周介质（CSM）相互作用产生的X射线和射电辐射特性，发现CSM几何形状（球形、沙漏形、盘形）显著影响射电光变曲线和X射线谱。基于这些特征，作者提出了利用射电光变曲线峰值附近上升段来约束CSM结构的方法，并将该方法成功应用于SN 1993j和SN 2023ixf，发现非对称CSM与观测高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超新星抛射物与致密星周介质的相互作用将激波动能转化为多波段辐射。理解CSM几何形状对X射线和射电辐射的影响，对于揭示超新星前身星的质量损失历史至关重要。

Method: 作者对球形、沙漏形和盘形三种CSM几何进行了系统模拟，分析X射线和射电的谱与光变曲线特征差异。他们研究了非球形CSM对自由-自由吸收（射电光变曲线峰值附近平坦化）和观测视线方向CSM密度的影响，并提出了通过射电光变曲线上升段约束CSM结构的方法，结合X射线信号进行交叉验证。

Result: 发现非球形CSM中射电光变曲线在峰值频率附近因未被激波冲击的CSM产生有效自由-自由吸收而变得平坦；观测视线方向CSM密度较高时，射电光变曲线早期上升更平缓；视线方向密度较低时，射电光变曲线在峰值处平坦化且X射线中反向激波成分可忽略。该方法在SN 1993j和SN 2023ixf上测试成功，非对称CSM与两者的射电和X射线观测均高度一致。

Conclusion: 非对称CSM结构为SN 1993j和SN 2023ixf的射电与X射线观测提供了优于文献中非风状场景的可行替代解释。射电和X射线信号为揭示超新星前身星的质量损失历史提供了关键洞察。

Abstract: The interaction of supernova (SN) ejecta with the dense circumstellar medium (CSM) converts shock kinetic energy into radiation across multiple wavebands. We investigate the dependence of the X-ray and radio emission on the CSM geometry, considering spherical, hourglass, and disk shapes for the CSM. We find that the spectral and light-curve properties, both in X-ray and radio, significantly differ for spherical and non-spherical CSM structures. For a non-spherical CSM, the radio light curve flattens out near the peak frequency, due to efficient free-free absorption by the unshocked CSM. Moreover, the early rise of the radio light curve is shallower when the CSM density along the observer line of sight is larger than that in other directions. If the CSM density is lower along the observer line of sight, the radio light curve flattens near its peak, and the reverse-shock component is negligible in X-rays. Building on these features, we provide a method to constrain the CSM structure based on the rising part the radio light curve in the proximity of its peak; we show that the decay part of the radio light curve, after its peak, carries insight on whether the CSM density profile is wind-like or not. We further adopt the X-ray signal to corroborate the information extracted from radio. We test our strategy on SN 1993j and SN 2023ixf. For both SNe, we find that an asymmetric CSM is in excellent agreement with radio and X-ray observations and provides a viable alternative to non-wind scenarios suggested in the literature. Our findings highlight the crucial insight provided by radio and X-ray signals into the mass-loss history of the SN progenitor.

</details>


### [124] [3D Radiative Transfer of Lyman-series Lines with SKIRT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12527)
*N. Sameshima,A. Lauwers,B. Vander Meulen,M. Tsujimoto,L. -Y. Gu,M. Baes,P. Camps*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文扩展了三维蒙特卡罗辐射传输代码SKIRT，实现了氢类离子Lyman系列谱线（Z=1–30，n≤10）的共振散射、辐射复合和偏振建模，并成功再现P Cygni轮廓等RT效应，为XRISM和IXPE时代的X射线光致电离等离子体诊断提供了强大工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XRISM和IXPE提供的高分辨率X射线光谱和偏振测量为致密天体周围光致电离等离子体的几何与运动学提供了新的诊断手段，但解释这些再处理X射线发射需要对包含光子-离子相互作用的全三维辐射传输（3D RT）进行建模，而传统的1D RT代码无法胜任。

Method: 在MC RT代码SKIRT中实现了Z=1–30氢类离子的Lyman系列跃迁（最高至n=10），包括精细结构劈裂和共振散射中的线性偏振；同时考虑了共振散射和辐射复合两种Lyman系列谱线产生通道。

Result: 实现结果与解析预期一致，并与Cloudy模拟结果吻合良好。SKIRT能自然捕捉光学厚介质中的P Cygni轮廓和谱线轮廓畸变等RT效应，这些是传统1D X射线RT代码无法获得的。在3D几何中，各向异性辐照和速度场显著改变Lyman系列谱线比和轮廓，均可用XRISM观测到。

Conclusion: 扩展版SKIRT为解释光致电离等离子体的X射线光谱和偏振提供了强大框架，特别适用于在XRISM和IXPE时代约束致密天体附近的几何和速度结构。

Abstract: Context. High-resolution X-ray spectroscopy and polarimetry provided by XRISM and IXPE offer new diagnostics of the geometry and kinematics of photo-ionised plasmas around compact objects. Interpreting reprocessed X-ray emission in such systems requires full three-dimensional radiative transfer (3D RT) including photon-ion interactions.
  Aims. We extend the Monte Carlo (MC) RT code SKIRT by implementing the Lyman-series lines of H-like ions, enabling self-consistent modelling of resonance scattering, radiative recombination, and polarisation of these lines in X-ray photo-ionised plasmas.
  Methods. We implemented Lyman-series transitions (up to $n=10$) for ions with $Z=$1--30, including fine-structure splitting and linear polarisation in resonance scattering. Two channels for the production of the Lyman-series lines (resonance scattering and radiative recombination) are considered.
  Results. The implementation reproduces analytical expectations and shows good agreement with Cloudy. The SKIRT simulations naturally capture RT effects such as P Cygni profiles and line-profile distortion in optically thick media, which are inaccessible to the conventional 1D RT codes commonly used in X-rays. In 3D geometries, we find that anisotropic illumination and velocity fields significantly modify the Lyman series line ratios and profiles, all of which are observable with XRISM.
  Conclusions. The extended version of SKIRT provides a powerful framework for interpreting X-ray line spectra and polarisation from photo-ionised plasmas. It is particularly suited for constraining the geometry and velocity structure in the vicinity of compact objects in the XRISM and IXPE era.

</details>


### [125] [How Neutron Star Radii Encode the Dense-Matter Equation of State and Hadron-Quark Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12632)
*Bao-An Li,Xavier Grundler*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来高精度中子星半径测量如何编码致密物质状态方程（EOS）的微观信息，特别聚焦于强子-夸克一级相变及其质量-半径拓扑结构。通过贝叶斯框架和九参数meta-model EOS，作者引入逆EOS-半径映射以量化每个EOS参数对半径测量的敏感性和精度依赖性，揭示出不同参数在半径编码中的信息层级：对称能参数L和K_sym被强烈编码，相变密度ρ_t次之，而更高阶强子参数和相变跳跃参数则更多依赖质量-半径序列的完整拓扑。结果表明由于不同拓扑的R_1.4分布高度重叠，即使高精度半径测量也无法单独识别拓扑或唯一确定高密度相变性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星半径测量是约束致密物质状态方程的关键观测手段。随着未来高精度观测（如NICER后续任务）即将到来，需要系统性地理解半径测量究竟能编码哪些微观EOS信息，以及这些信息在不同相变情景（特别是可能的强子-夸克一级相变）下如何分布，从而为观测任务设计和科学回报评估提供理论指导。

Method: 作者采用贝叶斯框架，使用具有九个微观参数的meta-model EOS集合，构造模拟的高斯型半径测量数据（R_1.4=11.9±σ_R km，σ_R=0.9和0.1 km）。核心创新是引入逆EOS-半径映射，给出每个EOS参数的后验均值作为R_1.4的函数，其斜率衡量半径敏感性，曲率通过Jensen展开决定后验均值的领先阶精度依赖性。同时将EOS按质量-半径拓扑分为Connected、Disconnected、Both和No-Quark-Matter四类，分别分析各参数在不同拓扑下的信息编码程度。

Result: 研究发现信息编码呈现清晰的层级结构：(1) 对称能参数L（斜率）和K_sym（曲率）被R_1.4强烈编码，其高精度测量可使后验均值显著移动；(2) 相变密度ρ_t是相变参数中最强编码的；(3) 能密度跳跃和夸克物质声速更多关联于完整质量-半径序列的拓扑而非R_1.4本身；(4) 高阶强子参数表现出更强的拓扑依赖性。关键局限在于四种拓扑的R_1.4分布高度重叠，因此即使极高精度的半径测量也无法单独区分拓扑或唯一确定相变性质。

Conclusion: 高精度半径测量对约束EOS低密度和中等密度部分（特别是对称能参数）具有高效率，但单独依赖R_1.4无法解码高密度相变信息。研究提供了参数依赖的信息层级排序，可用于评估未来高精度半径测量任务的科学回报，并强调了将半径测量与互补探针（如潮汐形变、引力波、X射线等）结合的必要性，以突破拓扑简并的限制。

Abstract: We investigate how future high-precision neutron star (NS) radius measurements encode microscopic information about the dense-matter equation of state (EOS), focusing on a possible first-order hadron--quark phase transition and the resulting mass--radius topology. Within a Bayesian framework using meta-model EOSs with nine microscopic parameters, we analyze mock radius measurements $R_{1.4}=11.9\pmσ_R$ km with $σ_R=0.9$ and $0.1$ km for canonical NSs. We introduce inverse EOS--radius mappings that give the posterior mean of each EOS parameter as a function of $R_{1.4}$. Their slope measures radius sensitivity, while their curvature determines the leading precision dependence of the posterior mean through the Jensen expansion. Resolving the mappings into four mass--radius topologies, Connected, Disconnected, Both, and No-Quark-Matter, reveals a clear hierarchy of information. The symmetry-energy parameters $L$ (slope) and $K_{\rm sym}$ (curvature) are strongly encoded in $R_{1.4}$ and their posterior means shift appreciably with improved radius precision, whereas the higher-order hadronic parameters show stronger topology dependence. Among the transition parameters, the transition density $ρ_t$ is the most strongly encoded in $R_{1.4}$, while the energy-density jump and quark-matter sound speed are more strongly associated with the topology of the full mass--radius sequence. Since the different topologies have strongly overlapping $R_{1.4}$ distributions, even precise radius measurements cannot by themselves identify the topology or uniquely determine the high-density transition properties. These results provide a parameter-dependent hierarchy for assessing the scientific return of future high-precision radius measurements and complementary probes of high-density

</details>


### [126] [An Evolving Leptonic Jet Model for Delayed Radio Flares in Neutrino Blazars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12696)
*Alina Kochocki,Xavier Rodrigues,Nathan Whitehorn*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空分辨的射电喷流演化模型，用于解释耀变体TXS 0506+056中与中微子事件相关的伽马射线耀发及其后三年达到峰值的延迟射电耀发现象。模型模拟了从亚秒差距到秒差距尺度的喷流中电子的多频段辐射演化，发现秒差距尺度喷流与较老、冷却电子群的相遇建立了高能发射与滞后射电活动之间的因果联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰立方中微子事件与耀变体（如TXS 0506+056）的关联暗示喷流中可能存在强子加速和中微子产生，但2017年中微子事件对应的伽马射线耀发与三年后达到峰值的射电耀发之间的时间延迟无法用传统的单区辐射模型解释，因为这类模型仅聚焦于紧凑的高能辐射区，忽略了喷流在空间上的延伸演化。

Method: 作者构建了一个将数值粒子相互作用框架与时空喷流演化动力学描述相结合的模型，计算了电子沿物理上延伸的喷流（直至秒差距尺度）的多频段辐射。通过将模型拟合到包括多频射电光变曲线在内的多波段观测数据，分析了TXS 0506+056的时域多频演化。

Result: 拟合结果表明，秒差距尺度的喷流与一群较老的、已冷却的电子在可观测射电核边缘附近相遇，从而在高能初始辐射与后续射电活动之间建立起因果联系。这一模型成功解释了伽马射线耀发与延迟数年的射电耀发之间的时间关联。

Conclusion: 这种空间和时间分辨的建模方法能够为耀发中微子候选耀变体的喷流物理条件提供新的见解，弥补了单区模型无法描述多波段长时间尺度演化（特别是延迟射电耀发）的不足，为理解喷流中粒子加速机制和位置提供了更全面的框架。

Abstract: The jets of blazar active galactic nuclei (AGN) are promising sites of hadron acceleration and subsequent neutrino production, owing to their extreme intrinsic power and high radiation density. Potential associations of IceCube neutrinos with blazars displaying delayed radio flares, such as TXS~0506+056 and PKS~1424+240, may support this scenario. However, the mechanisms and location of particle acceleration in the jet remain unclear. The 2017 IceCube event associated with TXS 0506+056 was concurrent with the initial peak of a flare in gamma rays, followed by a longer flare at radio frequencies peaking three years later. State-of-the-art single-zone radiative source models focus on the compact high-energy-emitting region, and thus fail to describe this subsequent radio enhancement. In this work, we model the time-domain multi-frequency evolution of TXS 0506+056 by considering electron emission along the physically extended jet. We couple a numerical particle interaction framework with a dynamic description of the temporal and spatial jet evolution down to the parsec scale. We fit the model to multi-wavelength data, including multi-frequency radio light curves. The results suggest that the parsec-scale jet meets a population of older, cooled electrons near the edge of the observable radio core, establishing a causal relation between the initial high-energy emission and trailing radio activity. This spatially and temporally resolved modeling approach can offer new insights into the physical conditions along the jet of flaring neutrino candidate blazars.

</details>


### [127] [Cross-spectral Analysis of the Type-C Quasi-periodic Oscillation Shoulder Component in GX 339-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12966)
*Haifan Zhu,Mariano Méndez,Pengcheng Yang,Pei Jin,Candela Bellavita,Federico García,Wei Wang,Diego Altamirano,Liang Zhang,Chenxu Gao,Xiao Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文重新分析了GX 339-4在2006/2007爆发上升期的RXTE观测数据，通过联合功率谱-交叉谱分解方法研究了type-C QPO区域。研究首次探测到QPO高频侧的一个肩状结构（shoulder），其频率随QPO演化（R_ν≈1.04-1.18），携带更大的硬滞后（~0.5-0.8 rad），并在谱滞后和rms-能量行为上与随后的type-B QPO相似，可能代表type-B QPO更早的宽频阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法只能单独分析功率谱或交叉谱，难以分离QPO基波与相邻的高频肩状结构这一复杂区域。作者希望通过联合PDS-CS分解方法，分别测量QPO基波和肩状结构的振幅与相位滞后，从而揭示type-C QPO区域的精细结构，并探究其与type-B QPO之间可能存在的物理联系。

Method: 采用联合功率谱密度-交叉谱分解方法，对GX 339-4 2006/2007爆发上升期的type-C QPO区域进行解耦分析，分别提取窄QPO基波与高频肩状结构的振幅和相位滞后；并利用分能量段拟合来研究二者的能量依赖关系。

Result: 肩状结构首次在MJD 54142.04通过交叉谱虚部和相位滞后谱的窄局部结构被探测到，随后在PDS中成为可分辨的高频肩。其频率比R_ν≈1.04-1.18；QPO滞后保持在~0.17 rad以下，而肩状结构带有~0.5-0.8 rad的较大硬滞后；肩状结构滞后随光子能量增加。二者rms-能量形状大致相似，但相对强度演化不同。肩状结构Q~2-4，其滞后和rms-能量行为与观测后不久探测到的type-B QPO相似。

Conclusion: 肩状结构可能代表了后来演变为type-B QPO的可变性的早期、更宽频阶段，这为理解type-C到type-B QPO之间的演化联系提供了新的观测线索。

Abstract: We revisit Rossi X-ray Timing Explorer (RXTE) observations of GX~339$-$4 during the rising phase of its 2006/2007 outburst and apply a joint power-density-spectrum (PDS)--cross-spectrum (CS) decomposition to the type-C quasi-periodic oscillation (QPO) region. Within this framework, the QPO region is described by a narrow QPO fundamental and a neighboring high-frequency shoulder, whose amplitudes and phase lags can be measured separately. The shoulder is first detected at MJD~54142.04, mainly through the imaginary part of the CS and a narrow local structure in the phase-lag spectrum, before becoming a resolved high-frequency shoulder in the PDS. It follows the QPO frequency evolution on the high-frequency side, with $R_ν=ν_{\rm sh}/ν_{\rm QPO}\simeq1.04$--$1.18$. The QPO lag remains small, typically below $\sim0.17$~rad, whereas the shoulder carries a larger hard lag of $\sim0.5$--$0.8$~rad. Energy-resolved fits show the same separation: the QPO lag is close to zero or only weakly positive across most of the energy band, while the shoulder lag is systematically larger and generally increases with photon energy. The two components have broadly similar rms--energy shapes, although their relative strengths evolve during the observed sequence. Although the shoulder remains broad, with $Q\sim2$--$4$, its lag and rms--energy behavior resemble those of the type-B QPO detected shortly after our observations. This similarity raises the interesting possibility that the shoulder is related to an earlier, broader stage of the variability later seen as the type-B QPO.

</details>


### [128] [AT 2024qfm: a luminous fast blue optical transient at a redshift of z = 0.2267 identified by Lasair-ZTF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13003)
*M. Fulton,S. J. Smartt,S. Srivastav,J. H. Gillanders,J. W. Tweddle,M. E. Huber,M. Nicholl,C. R. Angus,K. W. Smith,K. C. Chambers,A. Lawrence,R. Williams,D. R. Young,K. Auchettl,T. de Boer,T. -W. Chen,C. -H. Lai,C. C. Lin,G. S. H. Paek,M. Pursiainen,S. I. Raimundo,R. Wainscoat,S. Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了LFBOT（明亮快速蓝色光学暂现源）新成员AT 2024qfm的发现，位于红移z=0.2267的宿主星系中。该暂现源通过ZTF观测数据和Lasair broker中的自定义滤波器（FastFinder）被识别，其紫外到光学亮度及13天快速衰减特征与AT 2018cow高度吻合。作者还讨论了在Rubin天文台LSST巡天背景下，该发现方法（FastFinder）的扩展应用前景及多波段后续观测的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LFBOTs的物理起源仍存在争议，缺乏统一共识。截至目前仅有十余例被发现，样本量严重不足。要进一步约束理论模型，需要更多发现、宿主星系表征和多波段分析。本文旨在通过发现新事件AT 2024qfm，并展示一种可扩展的调查方法（FastFinder滤波器），为提高LFBOT发现率和后续研究能力做出贡献。

Method: 作者利用Zwicky Transient Facility（ZTF）的警报流，在Lasair broker中构建了一个自定义滤波器（FastFinder），通过在时间序列上检测通量梯度来识别快速演化的暂现源。该方法成功识别出AT 2024qfm并将其分类为LFBOT，同时以相同方法识别出另一个候选体AT 2024kth。作者还对发现效率进行了模拟，评估了在Rubin LSST巡天中该方法的恢复能力。

Result: AT 2024qfm在z=0.2267（±0.0002）的宿主星系中被确认，其紫外至光学光度及约13天的快速衰减与原型事件AT 2018cow高度匹配。FastFinder滤波器成功在ZTF警报流中识别该事件。模拟显示在LSST巡天中，该方法可恢复此类事件，且LSST的灵敏度将使LFBOT的有效巡天体积相对ZTF扩大十倍，延伸至z≲0.6。

Conclusion: FastFinder方法证明了在常规巡天数据流中高效发现快速演化暂现源的可行性。随着LSST时代的到来，LFBOT发现率将大幅提升，但关键挑战在于需要足够低的延迟来触发多波段后续观测，以约束理论模型。更多LFBOT样本将有助于解决其物理起源的争议。

Abstract: Luminous fast blue optical transients (LFBOTs) emit from x-ray to radio wavelengths, epitomised by the discovery of AT 2018cow in a host galaxy at 65 Mpc. In the following eight years eleven more have been found, at redshifts $0.075 \lesssim z \lesssim0.34$, plus one identified retrospectively from 2016. Here we present the discovery of AT 2024qfm, classified as an LFBOT in a host galaxy at $z = 0.2267 \pm 0.0002$. Its ultraviolet-to-optical luminosity and rapid 13 day fade closely match AT 2018cow. We describe how the transient was identified in the Zwicky Transient Facility alert stream using a custom filter in the Lasair broker that flags flux gradients over time. Another LFBOT candidate was identified with the same methodology (AT 2024kth). The physical origin of LFBOTs remains debated with no firm consensus, and further progress requires more discoveries, host-galaxy characterisation, and multi-wavelength analysis to constrain theory. We discuss this discovery in the context of Rubin Observatory's Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST), whose sensitivity will increase the effective LFBOT survey volume tenfold relative to ZTF, out to $z \lesssim 0.6$, and show that our FastFinder filter could recover such events. We highlight the challenge of detecting their fast evolution with sufficiently low latency to trigger multi-wavelength follow-up that can constrain theoretical models.

</details>


### [129] [Complex Energy-Dependent Behaviour of Quasi-Periodic Oscillation Observed in GRS 1915+105](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13068)
*Vaibhav Sharma,Ranjeev Misra,V. Jithesh,Shivani Chaudhary,Khushi Jirawala,J S Yadav,Pankaj Jain,N J Juris*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用AstroSat上的LAXPC仪器观测黑洞X射线双星GRS 1915+105，在不同能段构建功率密度谱（PDS）并多洛伦兹分量建模，发现QPO频率随能量轻微增加，但动态PDS未显示时间演化证据。双洛伦兹分量模型优于单分量，且两分量在分数均方根振幅和相位滞后上表现出不同行为，表明观测到的QPO能量依赖演化特征可能是多个可变性分量叠加的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞X射线双星中准周期振荡（QPO）的能量依赖特性，以理解其物理起源。通过分析QPO频率随能量的变化，判断该现象是否由单一物理机制（如时间演化或能量依赖）驱动，抑或是多个可变性分量叠加的结果。

Method: 使用AstroSat/LAXPC观测数据，在多能量波段构建PDS并用多洛伦兹分量建模；进行动态PDS分析以检查时间演化；对能量分辨PDS进行同时拟合；同时对PDS与交叉谱的实部和虚部建模以研究相位滞后性质。

Result: QPO频率随能量轻微增加；动态PDS无明显QPO频率时间演化证据；双洛伦兹分量模型拟合优于单分量；两分量在分数均方根振幅随能量的演化上不同；两分量表现出不同的相位滞后行为。

Conclusion: 观测到的QPO能量依赖演化特征表明存在多于一个可变性分量，而非由单纯的频率时间演化或单一物理过程驱动，这为理解QPO在黑洞吸积系统中的产生机制提供了新的线索。

Abstract: We present complex energy-resolved properties of quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) in the black hole X-ray binary GRS 1915+105 using an observation from the LAXPC instrument onboard AstroSat. Power density spectra (PDSs) are constructed in multiple energy bands and modeled with multi-Lorentzian components to investigate the energy dependence of QPO properties. The QPO frequency shows a modest increase with energy. Dynamic PDS analysis does not reveal clear evidence for time-dependent evolution of the QPO frequency, suggesting that the observed frequency shift is not primarily driven by temporal variability. We perform simultaneous fitting of energy-resolved PDSs and find that a model in which the QPO feature is described by two Lorentzian components provides a better fit. The two components exhibit different evolution in fractional root mean square amplitude as a function of energy. We further examine the phase-lag properties by simultaneously modeling the PDS and the real and imaginary parts of the cross-spectrum and find distinct phase-lag behavior for the two components. Overall, these results indicate that the apparent energy-dependent evolution of the QPO feature may be a result of the presence of more than one variability component.

</details>


### [130] [The low luminosity end of Galactic HMXBs with eROSITA: Establishing a luminosity floor for accreting BeXRBs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13259)
*Aafia Zainab,Philipp Thalhammer,Nico Zalot,Artur Avakyan,Jakob Stierhof,Ekaterina Sokolova-Lapa,Victor Doroshenko,Victoria Grinberg,Peter Kretschmar,Galina Lipunova,Nazma Islam,Matthias R. Schreiber,Christian Kirsch,Steven Hämmerich,Philipp Weber,Ralf Ballhausen,Alicia Rouco Escorial,Joel Coley,Richard Rothschild,Katja Pottschmidt,Joern Wilms*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统展示了SRG/eROSITA前四次巡天观测到的银河系已知高能X射线双星（HMXBs）群体的结果。eROSITA的灵敏度首次在大面积巡天中覆盖到10^32-10^34 erg/s的光度范围，作者给出了延伸的log N-log S分布、亚型分辨的光度分布，并基于观测证据提出Be X射线双星（BeXRBs）在爆发期外发生吸积可能是常态（>80%被探测到），而非个别例外，同时讨论了冷盘吸积和"螺旋桨"机制，并发现了一小群"孤立"Be星可能拥有致密伴星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往大面积巡天仪器的灵敏度不足以探测银河系中大多数HMXBs的低光度状态（特别是BeXRB在爆发期外的行为），这一直是长期未解的问题。eROSITA前所未有的灵敏度（~10^-13 erg s^-1 cm^-2）首次允许在宽视场巡天中系统研究这一光度区间，填补了该领域的观测空白。

Method: 利用SRG/eROSITA前四次巡天数据，对已知HMXBs进行系统检测和光度测量；构建扩展的log N-log S分布；按亚型（SgXBs与BeXRBs）分别统计光度分布；重分析RXTE/ASM和MAXI历史数据与eROSITA结果进行交叉对比；在冷盘吸积和"螺旋桨"机制的框架下解释观测到的低光度行为。

Result: eROSITA首次在宽视场巡天中探测到HMXBs光度的10^32 erg/s量级区间；SgXBs和BeXRBs在总光度分布中占据不同区域；超过80%的BeXRBs在爆发期外被检测到，其光度比最X射线明亮的Be星预期值高至少一个数量级；发现一小群"孤立"Be星的光度可与最低光度的BeXRBs相比，暗示其可能存在致密伴星。

Conclusion: 作者提出，BeXRBs在爆发期外的吸积很可能是普遍规律而非例外行为（由eROSITA的大面积样本支持）；并指出存在一类先前未被识别的"孤立"Be星群体可能隐藏着未探测到的致密天体伴星。

Abstract: We present a first look at the Galactic population of heretofore known HMXBs as observed by SRG/eROSITA during its first four surveys. eROSITA's sensitivity of $\sim10^{-13}\,\mathrm{erg}\,\mathrm{s}^{-1}\,\mathrm{cm}^{-2}$, translating to $10^{32}$-$10^{34},\mathrm{erg}\,\mathrm{s}^{-1}$ in luminosity for most known HMXBs in the Milky Way, has thus far never been reached by any wide-area survey instrument. We present the extended log N-log L distribution of known HMXBs reaching down to $10^{32}\,\mathrm{erg}\,\mathrm{s}^{-1}$ using eROSITA, and show the large scatter that can be induced by source intrinsic variability. We present sub-type resolved luminosity distributions, showing that the Supergiant X-ray binaries (SgXBs) and Be X-ray binaries (BeXRBs) occupy different parts of the overall distribution, and reanalyse RXTE/ASM data and MAXI for comparison to eROSITA. The luminosity regime uncovered by eROSITA allows a systematic study of the "transient" BeXRBs, which are typically below the detection threshold of monitors outside of outburst, and whose low luminosity behavior has been a longstanding question. Signatures of stable accretion at low luminosities have been observed with pointed instruments for a fraction of the overall sample, so far. With the eROSITA results, we posit that accretion outside of outburst is likely the norm, since a vast majority (> 80%) of BeXRBs are detected at luminosities at least an order of magnitude higher than expected for the most X-ray luminous Be stars. We discuss the observed luminosity in the context of cold disk accretion and the "propeller" mechanism. We highlight a small subpopulation of "isolated" Be-stars that reach luminosities comparable to the least luminous BeXRBs, hinting at the presence of compact object companions.

</details>


### [131] [X-ray Flaring and Variability in NGC 1275, the Heart of the Perseus Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13281)
*Sarah Ketchum,Jon M. Miller,S. W. Allen,Doyee Byun,Tianyin Hu,Missagh Mehdipour,Veronica Tananko,Xin Xiang,Irina Zhuravleva*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用尼尔·盖尔雷斯·斯威夫特天文台20年的X射线监测数据，对NGC 1275（英仙座星系团中心星系）的AGN进行了高精度监测，在2023年2月探测到约2倍的X射线耀发，并在约300天后于43 GHz射电波段发现对应耀发，可能在星系尺度上建立盘-喷流联系的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NGC 1275中的活动星系核（AGN）产生射电喷流并在星系团热介质中充胀大气泡，对大规模结构产生显著影响。然而，高分辨率X射线成像虽能分离AGN与明亮的星系团介质，但要监测黑洞吸积率并研究盘-喷流联系，需要高时域观测，现有研究缺乏这种长期高节奏的X射线监测。

Method: 利用Swift卫星20年的X射线观测数据对NGC 1275进行监测。在每次观测中对时域恒定的星系团内介质进行建模并扣除，从而可靠提取黑洞吸积产生的X射线发射，典型流量误差约3%。将X射线耀发事件与43 GHz射电监测数据进行比对分析。

Result: 在MJD 59956（2023年2月21日）起探测到持续数天的约2倍X射线耀发，其发射区尺度限制在r≤870(10^8 M_⊙/M_BH) GM/c²范围内。耀发形态与潮汐瓦解事件的简单预言不符。在X射线耀发约300天后，43 GHz射电监测中也出现对应耀发，暗示喷流重新活动的时间延迟。

Conclusion: 结果表明，协调的中等分辨率X射线成像与射电监测可以追踪对大规模结构影响最显著的AGN中的盘-喷流联系，该方法可推广至其他影响宿主天体的源。

Abstract: NGC 1275 is the central galaxy in the Perseus Cluster. The active galactic nucleus (AGN) within NGC 1275 is notable for its strong and variable radio activity, tied to the production of radio jets that inflate large bubbles in the hot intracluster medium (ICM). High spatial resolution X-ray imaging can separate the AGN from the bright ICM, but monitoring the mass accretion rate onto the black hole and establishing disk-jet connections in NGC 1275 requires a high cadence. Here, we report on X-ray monitoring of NGC 1275 using data taken over 20 years with the Neil Gehrels Swift Observatory. Modeling the temporally constant ICM in each observation allows X-ray emission from accretion onto the black hole to be traced reliably, with typical flux errors of $\sim 3\%$. X-ray flaring by a factor of $\sim2$ over mere days is detected starting on MJD 59956 (2023 Feb. 21). The flares imply an emission region consistent with $r \leq 870~(10^{8}~M_{\odot}/M_{BH})~ GM/c^{2}$. The profile of the flaring is inconsistent with simple predictions for tidal disruption events. A flare appears roughly 300 days later in radio monitoring data at 43 GHz. Overall, our results indicate that coordinated, moderate-resolution X-ray imaging and radio monitoring could potentially trace disk-jet connections in the AGN that most vividly impact large-scale structure, and be extended to other sources that impact their hosts.

</details>


### [132] [Radio Monitoring of Classical Novae using the ASKAP Variable and Slow Transients Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13330)
*Aishani Majumder,David L. Kaplan,Laura N. Driessen,Ashna Gulati,Tara Murphy,Dougal Dobie*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用澳大利亚SKA探路者（ASKAP）可变及暂现源巡天（VAST）在887.5 MHz频率上对经典新星进行射电搜寻，在43颗光学发现的新星中检测到3颗具有显著射电辐射（V6598 Sgr、V1716 Sco和V1723 Sco）。通过热自由-自由和非热同步加速两种发射模型对射电光变曲线进行MCMC拟合，发现三颗新星均以非热同步加速辐射为主导机制，支持激波驱动的粒子加速，且与伽马射线探测相关。研究采用分段幂律描述非均匀环双星物质密度结构，表明即使仅基于单频射电光变曲线，结合物理动机模型仍能有效约束主导发射机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典新星在射电波段的辐射探测有助于理解其喷出物与周围介质的相互作用，但此前研究多依赖多频数据来区分热与非热发射模型。本文旨在探索仅利用单频射电光变曲线是否足以区分主导发射机制，并寻找新星射电辐射与伽马射线激波加速之间的关联。

Method: 使用ASKAP VAST巡天在1200平方度银道巡天覆盖范围内，交叉匹配2021年9月至2025年11月期间爆发的43颗光学发现的新星，并通过两种物理模型（热自由-自由和非热同步加速）进行拟合，采用MCMC方法约束参数。对于非热模型，采用分段幂律描述非均匀环双星物质的径向密度结构。

Result: 从43颗新星中检测到3颗具有显著射电辐射（V6598 Sgr、V1716 Sco和V1723 Sco），三颗均显示非热同步加速辐射为该频率下的主导发射机制。分段幂律密度剖面比标准风密度剖面提供更好的拟合，且三颗新星均有伽马射线探测，支持激波驱动粒子加速的解释。

Conclusion: 单频射电光变曲线在结合物理动机模型的情况下，仍能有效区分主导发射机制并约束新星喷出物和周围介质的物理参数。早期强同步加速辐射的普遍存在表明激波驱动粒子加速在新星射电辐射中可能比以往认识的更为普遍，与伽马射线探测存在内在关联。

Abstract: We present a search for radio emission from classical novae at 887.5 MHz using data from the Australian SKA Pathfinder Variable And Slow Transient (VAST) survey. We cross-matched 43 optically discovered classical novae that erupted between 2021 September and 2025 November within the 1200-square-degree Galactic survey footprint, and found three which show significant radio emission: V6598 Sgr, V1716 Sco, and V1723 Sco. To analyse their radio light curves, we use both thermal free-free and non-thermal synchrotron emission models. We fit the data using the Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) method to constrain parameters, including the ejected mass and ejecta velocities for the thermal models, and mass-loss rate, explosion energy, wind velocity, and filling factor for the non-thermal model. All three novae show evidence of non-thermal synchrotron emission as the dominant emission mechanism at this frequency. We use a broken power law to describe the radial density structure of a non-uniform circumbinary material, which provides a better fit than a standard wind density profile. This strong early-time synchrotron emission is strong evidence of shock-driven particle acceleration, which may be related to detections of gamma-rays from all three novae as well. In contrast to earlier studies that used multi-frequency data to distinguish between emission models, our analysis is based on single-frequency radio light curves, which can still provide useful constraints on the dominant emission mechanism when interpreted with physically motivated models

</details>
